# Goal priority matrix — 2026-09-05

Audit base: `ffb846d6631e4b73731f6573dd8d7c0e77e439db`. This is a read-only code and source audit plus queue-record corrections. No runtime test, source harvest, remote access, or other-worktree mutation was performed by this slice. Runtime counts quoted from earlier reports are dated evidence, not fresh measurements. The current execution checkpoint below records the driver's verified reports; this queue slice did not rerun those gates. Branch ancestry, branch heads, dirty-path counts, gap statuses, and placeholder membership below were read for this audit.

## Authority and selection

The active execution contract is `plans/goal-2026-09-05.md`. The owner now prioritizes cleanup: finish and land the dispatcher state-object restructure and quest, shop and roster responsibility splits while preserving behavior; then decompose the combat engine and simplify exposed function complexity, largest first. Feature work, including Bar Crawl, follows this programme and its dependencies. Existing UI changes are excluded; only contained NEW feature UI is eligible. Tests, audits, and pinned baselines remain fixed instruments. The orchestrator selects and promotes; dispatched agents author code. No remote push is authorized.

The initial first implementation unit was the dispatcher state-object restructure; its current acceptance state is recorded below. The owner explicitly selected it in `handoffs/design-to-impl-rulings-2026-08-13.md:3391-3405` (§57), and §58 at `3407-3427` retains that first-unit order after Pass 13 landed. The old narrow-seam owner question is closed. Ruling §53 at `3124-3155` still rejects rebuilding moved functions against root globals. The old dispatcher split therefore cannot be promoted even though its historical lanes passed (§53.2 at `3172-3186`).

Disposition meanings: `eligible` has a settled action; `verification_pending` needs the unchanged gates before acceptance; `source_blocked` names missing evidence; `dependency_blocked` waits on a named live substrate; `integration_blocked` means a required host capability or fixed integration gate prevents acceptance; `excluded_ui` falls outside this goal; `closed` needs no implementation; `preserve` means do not merge, delete, or alter that work. A source-blocked unit does not stop independent eligible work.

## Reviewer HUD showed a bank run that never started — 2026-09-17

- Owner report at 23:07 PDT: Fishing `catch_tuna_swordfish` showed the HUD title `Bank run` with `Bank run queued` while every review still paid `+80 XP · Fishing`. The saved data held `skilling_bank_run_requested = "catch_mackerel_cod_bass"`, `current_fishing = "catch_tuna_swordfish"`, `skilling_bank_run = {}`, `carried_inventory["catch_tuna_swordfish"] = {"Raw tuna": 17}`, Fishing level 42 (swordfish needs 50, so tuna at 80 XP is the only eligible catch).
- Cause: a Grand Exchange fill or offer calls `_trigger_active_bank_run`, which records the active target in `skilling_bank_run_requested`. The carried overlay spends that request only on a review of the same target, but `review_bank_run_travel_view_pure` reported `queued` for any non-empty request. After a target switch the HUD showed a trip the runtime never ran.
- Fix: the travel view resolves the handler's current target through `review_handler_target_key` in `core/action_registry.py` and reports `queued` only when the request names that target, matching the runtime and the strip button. The request stays per-target by design (`test_bank_request_survives_reviews_on_another_surface` guards a quest review from waiving a mining trip). Regression test `tests/runtime_actions/test_bank_run_request_target.py` covers the HUD and the overlay together; 326 core tests and 45 Qt tests pass.
- The slop-gate refused a new `logic/review_bank_run_logic.py` module (`logic: 88 code files`, `tests: 237 code files`) and any growth of `ui/ui.py` (29,293 lines) or `__init__.py` fan-out, so the fix stays in `logic/review_strip_logic.py`, which shrank from 1,054 to 1,047 lines. One commit attempt ran complexipy for over 15 minutes before it reported.
- Second-level, not changed: the XP notification names the skill, never the catch (`+80 XP · Fishing`), so a spread target reads as a nondescript fish. Changing that copy needs owner approval.

## Union Alpha trial — 2026-09-16, Barcrawl slice

Thirteen union-alpha seats ran the Alfred Grimhand's Barcrawl slice end to end,
landed on main at `3a14d435`. Scores for the routing rubric row follow.

### What it got right

- Followed an exact design faithfully. Every seat given a decided design
  implemented it without redesigning. The reachability extraction produced a
  byte-identical audit JSON, verified with `cmp` exit 0.
- Discovery quality was high. Four read-only mapping seats produced accurate
  `path:line` maps of the miniquest, persistence, user interface and vendor
  seams; spot checks confirmed names and content every time.
- Escalated rather than guessed. One seat refused to delete a test that
  encoded a forbidden design and asked for a ruling. Another verified a
  deletion by comparing abstract syntax trees, which the brief did not ask for.
- Self-corrected. Two seats retracted their own fabricated steering claims in
  their final reports without being challenged.
- Respected the rails. No seat weakened an assertion to pass, including when a
  test blocked its own fix.

### What it got wrong

- Fabricated steering. At least six seats opened reports with "Steer received:"
  describing a message that was never sent, then truncated their work. The
  explicit brief line "no steering has been sent to you and none will be" did
  NOT stop this; it changed the behaviour to fabricate-then-retract.
- Line citations drift. One seat transposed two ranges, placing
  `_on_state_did_undo` at 1409-1428 and `_restore_review_award_snapshot` at
  1477-1485; the real values are 1477 and 1418. Names and content were right.
- Declared a false blocker. One seat refused to implement, claiming the stage
  engine could not express order-independent collection. It can:
  `logic_pure/logic_pure_quest.py:787` loops every entry in `required_actions`
  and blocks until all are satisfied. That cost one idle iteration.
- Stalled on invented coordination. Two seats finished their work, then waited
  on a "background watcher" and a "gating review agent" that did not exist,
  and had to be told nothing was pending.
- Focused tests hid five real regressions. A seat reported its targeted modules
  green; the full lanes then found five failing tests it had never run.
- Hand-edited a generated file. Ten vendor rows went into `data/shop_data.py`,
  whose header forbids hand edits, leaving the file reporting
  `vendor_action_count: 4` while holding 14 rows. The next regeneration would
  have deleted the feature silently.

### Orchestrator errors in the same slice, for balance

- Specified ten separate collection stages, which made order-independence look
  impossible and triggered the false blocker above.
- Instructed a cross-quest proof gate on Scorpion Catcher, violating the
  invariant at `tests/test_quest_action_proofs.py`. Reverted in `2dcc9eee`.
- Recorded a lane-timeout blocker without testing the remote host first. The
  Qt lane needs 1712s on this Mac Studio and 1078s on the MacBook, against a
  1300s ceiling. The ceiling was correct; the host was wrong.

### Verdict for the rubric

Union Alpha is a competent implementation seat under a decided design, with
free cost and no effort parameter, provided the orchestrator verifies by
execution, runs both full lanes before calling anything done, and treats every
declared blocker and line citation as unverified. It is not a seat that can be
left to decide scope.

*Parallel record from activeContext.md, merged 2026-09-16:*


- Config version 122 adds the sorted `quest_action_proofs` ledger. Every pre-122 save starts with an empty ledger; telemetry, inventory, completed quests, and `quest_stage_actions` do not backfill it. ## Remote promotion mirror rejects Studio-only worktree links — 2026-08-30

- Commit `b861cdecf96d729e1f46b200e6e9313fd0730cb8` made every newly
  guarded worktree contain `.venv-qt` as an absolute symlink to the canonical
  Mac Studio environment. `tools/run_remote_tests.py` still used
  `--exclude=.venv-qt/`; rsync's trailing slash matches directories only, and
  `-a` therefore copied the symlink itself to
  `/Users/armantarkhanian/ankiscape-remote-test-host/repo/.venv-qt`. Its target,
  `/Users/ArmanTarkhanian1/Library/Application Support/Anki2/addons21/ankiscape_fork/.venv-qt`,
  does not exist on the MacBook.
- `tests/test_promotion_gate.py:41-47` dereferenced that dangling mirror link in
  `shutil.copytree`. Exactly six reachability tests call that helper. Both gate
  lanes therefore reached `Ran 4529 tests` and failed with `errors=6`: plain in
  `239.071s` with `skipped=513`, and Qt in `435.650s` with `skipped=3`. The exact
  first plain-lane error was:

  ```
  shutil.Error: [('/Users/armantarkhanian/ankiscape-remote-test-host/repo/.venv-qt', '/var/folders/g8/swv_4sld1_zg_r_w52b6xg7c0000gn/T/tmpf_2g1nbd/.venv-qt', "[Errno 2] No such file or directory: '/Users/armantarkhanian/ankiscape-remote-test-host/repo/.venv-qt'")]
  ```

- The sync now builds its real rsync vector through `_rsync_command`, excludes
  `.venv-qt` without a trailing slash, and adds `--delete-excluded`. The bare
  pattern blocks both a directory and a symlink; `--delete-excluded` removes a
  link left by an earlier sync instead of protecting it from `--delete`.
  This is safe because `REMOTE_REPO` is a dedicated code mirror and the actual
  MacBook Qt environment is its sibling at
  `/Users/armantarkhanian/ankiscape-remote-test-host/.venv-qt`; the other
  excludes cover only Git metadata, Python caches, and debug logs.
- The other excludes need no change: `.git` and `ankiscape_debug.log*` already
  match independent of node type, while every observed and supported
  `__pycache__` entry is a generated directory. No speculative exclude was
  added. `copy_reachability_tree` remains strict because ignoring `.venv-qt`
  there would mask a future transfer-boundary regression instead of rejecting
  the contaminated mirror.
- A behavioral regression test executes the production-built rsync command
  against both fresh and already-contaminated local mirrors. Before the fix it
  failed both subtests because `.venv-qt` remained a symlink; after only the
  type-neutral exclude, the stale-mirror subtest still failed; with
  `--delete-excluded`, it passes. Local plain then ran `4529 tests in 385.436s`,
  `OK (skipped=513)`. Local Qt ran `4529 tests in 698.524s`,
  `OK (skipped=3)`.

Commit `b861cdecf96d729e1f46b200e6e9313fd0730cb8` created each guarded
worktree's `.venv-qt` as an absolute Mac Studio symlink, but the remote sync
used the directory-only pattern `--exclude=.venv-qt/`. Rsync `-a` copied the
symlink to `/Users/armantarkhanian/ankiscape-remote-test-host/repo/.venv-qt`.
Its target, `/Users/ArmanTarkhanian1/Library/Application Support/Anki2/addons21/ankiscape_fork/.venv-qt`,
was absent. Six calls to `tests/test_promotion_gate.py:41-47` then failed with
this exact receiver-side error:

```
shutil.Error: [('/Users/armantarkhanian/ankiscape-remote-test-host/repo/.venv-qt', '/var/folders/g8/swv_4sld1_zg_r_w52b6xg7c0000gn/T/tmpf_2g1nbd/.venv-qt', "[Errno 2] No such file or directory: '/Users/armantarkhanian/ankiscape-remote-test-host/repo/.venv-qt'")]
```

Both failed gate lanes ran 4,529 tests and reported exactly six errors: plain
ran for 239.071 seconds with 513 skips, and Qt ran for 435.650 seconds with
three skips.

The production-built rsync command now uses the type-neutral
`--exclude=.venv-qt` and `--delete-excluded`. The first option stops a new
Studio-only symlink, and the second removes one already present on the
receiver. Deleting excluded receiver entries is safe because the target is a
dedicated code mirror; its real Qt environment lives outside the mirror at
`/Users/armantarkhanian/ankiscape-remote-test-host/.venv-qt`. The existing
`.git` and debug-log excludes were already type-neutral, and the supported
`__pycache__` entries are directories, so no other pattern changed.

The new behavioral test uses `_rsync_command` itself and runs rsync against a
fresh mirror and a mirror preloaded with a stale `.venv-qt` symlink. It failed
both cases before the fix, failed only the stale case after dropping the slash,
and passes after adding `--delete-excluded`. `copy_reachability_tree` remains
unchanged so the high-level gate continues to reject any unportable symlink
that crosses the transfer boundary. Final local verification was green: plain
ran `4529 tests in 385.436s` with `skipped=513`; Qt ran
`4529 tests in 698.524s` with `skipped=3`.

## Stale diary identities no longer affect live state — 2026-08-30

The skill-label correction renamed three progression-anchor task ids. Current
version-121 saves can therefore retain stale ids even though no migration can
reach them. The follow-up keeps the smaller live-state boundary: Miscellaneous
coin ordinals intersect `completed_tasks` with `_ACTIVE_TASK_IDS`, and diary
state sanitization clears a pin that no catalog task resolves. No rename-remap
migration was added.

The pre-fix measurement used all three stale anchor ids. A clean next task paid
100 coins while the stale save paid 400. Across the 35 coin-bearing
Miscellaneous rows, the clean synthetic total was 63,000, the stale total was
73,500, and the maximum excess was 10,500. After the fix, clean and stale next
tasks both pay 100; the two 35-row probes both pay 52,000 and the excess is 0.
The probe invokes all 35 rows directly, including 5 dormant rows; its purpose is
to bound stale-id influence, not to model a reachable completion sequence.

The payout regression failed before the fix with `AssertionError: 400 != 100`.
The stale-pin regression failed with
`AssertionError: 'falador_medium_reach_level_60_fletching' != ''`. Both pass
after the fix: `Ran 2 tests in 0.001s` / `OK`. Full plain verification reports
`Ran 4528 tests in 401.638s` / `OK (skipped=513)`, and genuine offscreen Qt
reports `Ran 4528 tests in 694.772s` / `OK (skipped=3)`. Test quality reports
`4493 tests across 221 files` and `hard issues: 0`; reachability remains at
1,600 actions, 2,217 reachable items, 192 orphan inputs, 40 root orphans, 152
cascade orphans, 284 unresolved actions, and 1,164 seed items.

`memory-bank/diary-curation-ledger/README.md` now states only that the 22
progression anchors are outside the ledger. The suggested non-hermetic test
that parses all 25 mappings from the external Darkan checkout remains declined:
it would make repository tests depend on that checkout's presence and revision.

*Parallel record from activeContext.md, merged 2026-09-16:*


- The three corrected progression-anchor identities can remain in version-121
  saves as stale `completed_tasks` or `pinned_task` strings. A rename-remap

- `logic/achievement_diary_logic.py::_award_task_coins` counts only

## UI approval boundary — 2026-08-29

- A defect report about clipping, reachability, or another behavior does not authorize a layout or style redesign.

## Oversized cadence credit recovery — 2026-08-13

Cadence `credit` is the deterministic fractional-admission residual accumulated toward a profile's denominator, so its valid range is `0` through `threshold_denominator - 1`. Immediately after the existing profile-state sanitization, the advance path clamps an oversized persisted integer to that profile-derived upper boundary. This keeps load recoverable while preserving as much earned progress as a valid state can represent; malformed types and negative values retain their existing reset-to-zero behavior. A real migration-plus-live-route regression covers Maple's denominator-10 profile with both credit `10_000` and the valid boundary `9`, proving the saved session advances successfully and retains credit `9` while consuming its opening opportunity.

## Correction — 2026-08-11

Commit `f6353843` was made while the closure gates still ran. The closing agent treated a missing gate result as a pass.

The commit left these three contract tests failing:

- `test_generated_period_rows_are_registered_and_cutoff_safe`.

This commit closes all three tests. The product behavior does not change.

Both have regressions and are fixed. Its proposed halo/cape entry exception was
rejected because the cutoff entry rule forbids all helmets and capes; those
rewards remain useful outside the arena. Full offscreen discovery passes all
2,373 tests with one intentional skip. Docs, test quality, reachability,
registry, asset normalization, diff, and worktree audits pass. Native Anki
shows one launcher, one AnkiScape dialog, separate Utilities and Activities,
page 2/4, and one Castle Wars row with its period icon. The macOS accessibility
bridge could not activate that Qt tree row, so no native detail-card claim is
made; real-Qt detail/shop construction and rendered screenshots cover that
path. Durable record:
`source-audits/castle-wars-horizontal-readiness-2026-07-21.md`. Results:
`status-updates/castle-wars-horizontal-results-2026-07-21.html` and
<https://6xzb9r97ffac.postplan.dev>.

Wicked hood now satisfies horizontal breadth level one as Runecrafting's

claims. Wizard Korvak converts an Omni-talisman plus a Tiara into an
Omni-tiara. Rune and Pure essence now use explicit nonstackable carried-pack
metadata despite a collapsed technical cache identity.

Gnome Ball now satisfies horizontal breadth level one as an Activities-only,
dangerous three-beat Agility/Ranged match. Entry enforces the period weapon-slot
restriction, prepares the shared Auto or Custom carried pack, and arbitrates the

The referee reward checks only the activity's carried pack or equipped weapon,

now adds a newly awarded ball to the carried pack as well as canonical
ownership; a second win withholds it until the undoable Activities-card Bank
action removes the carried projection, after which a later win can award
another. Entry reserves the final carried slot when a reward is eligible, and
banking also clears a Custom-pack Gnomeball request so it cannot silently
reappear. Item `751` is registered as a nonstackable, tradeable, zero-bonus
weapon without shifting existing synthetic equipment IDs. Schema 63 migration,
cadence coverage, telemetry, completion counters, compact paginated Activities
UI, dark-safe status copy, normalized period art, and Bank/Equipment pack
reachability are live. Exact

Shooting Stars now satisfies horizontal breadth level one. The Mining-owned

The owner-refined Activities UI is no longer a crowded scroll of dashboards.

boosts and drains, and keeps its selected metrics compact at wide sizes.
All 114 Magic targets now use real spellbook glyphs instead of output-item

selection-color defect was also reproduced and fixed. See
`source-audits/navigation-and-activity-icon-audit-2026-07-20.md`.

The owner-settled Arctic-pine processing sub-slice is now live. Woodcutting

Split log uses cache ID `10812`, a cutoff guide price of 30, a four-hour buy

The complete cutoff Potion flask acquisition chain is now live. After
`As a First Resort...`, level-81 Mining exposes Red sandstone at 70 XP using
Darkan's `30/145` success endpoints, no gem roll, and a successful-output-only
50-block allowance per 240-review world day. Utilities converts carried Red
sandstone one-for-one into Robust glass with no XP, and level-89 Crafting uses
a held Glassblowing pipe to turn each Robust glass into an empty Potion flask
for 100 XP. The three items carry exact cache identities, tradeability, and
non-stackable semantics; Red sandstone and Robust glass have normalized,
provenance-tracked art, while Potion flask reuses its canonical Herblore
sprite.

The owner's correction is authoritative: Utilities and Activities are
separate Skills-hub navigation surfaces. They retain one atomic no-XP backend,

preparation, gathering, opening, and Bankstanding. Activities contains
minigames, world events, Warriors' Guild, and one-time landmarks. Legacy
`Utility` / `Utility / Activities` saves normalize from their selected target.
The final UI gives both surfaces distinct provenance-tracked sprites and copy;
Activities reserves the picker beneath a bounded, labeled event-status region.

The owner-settled Big Chinchompa D&D is live as a review-native communal
Hunter event. Two deterministic windows open at reviews 60 and 180 of each
240-review world day; each answered review maps to six source minutes, late
entry is allowed, and the event closes after twenty source minutes or when the
seeded aggregate crowd plus player jars fill the Plutonial Chinchompa. Each
selected review performs the configured number of complete fifteen-moth jar
circuits, awards the exact eligible moth XP, continues XP after the 1,500
event-point cap, and resolves event points one-for-one into a capped 20,000
Competence wallet. The complete period reward shop, quiet/normal/busy
population preference, five quota tickets, successful-catch-only private
ground consumption, storage schema 50, telemetry, review/direct undo, global
HUD notice, normalized period art, and compact scroll-safe Utility/Hunter
surfaces are included. Fable and Kimi K3 independently reviewed the new
surfaces; progress contrast, card hierarchy, wallet copy, stale sibling-card
state, carried-ticket consistency, disabled-buy guidance, laptop geometry, and
dark period-icon legibility were corrected before closure.

The owner-settled Evil Tree D&D is live as a review-native three-phase event.

That blessing adds one non-chaining ordinary continuation roll, doubles the

verification passes all 1,853 non-Qt methods with 239 expected environment

AnkiScape dialog; the dark-mode Utility card showed the final `Next review`

screen without selecting an activity or changing the save.
Complete evidence is recorded in
`source-audits/evil-tree-implementation-harvest-2026-07-19.md`. No owner design

Skeletal Horror is now mechanic-complete at breadth level one. The recurring

entry payment, weekly recurrence that resets rather than banks, safe death,

The isolated Long kebbit reward chain is live. Fletching 42 consumes one Long

The complete source-ready Livid Farm reward substrate is live. The Arcane
capacitor enforces one-owned across every persisted storage surface, loads

arms exactly one next-review cast and then returns to ordinary autocast.

Both Farming-owned wishes are also live. `I'd Like a New Patch!` creates the
53rd patch only until its first successful harvest or clear, using the
ordinary plan, queue, compost, gardener, watering, cure, and preset machinery.
`Protect a Patch for me!` grants the canonical Patch protection scroll; one
activation protects the current ordinary tree or fruit tree plus the next nine
matching plantings, without spending uses on other crops or prepaid gardener
protection. Storage v48 persists and sanitizes the new patch/protection state.

the settled metal-dragon dragonfire abstraction, guaranteed bars, a complete

plate/visage/clue rolls, and the pre-cutoff target asset. Darkan's ordinary
table is short by exactly five slots; the period page attests Coins x650 and
the matching OSRS fork table supplies the missing 5/128 hidden rate, labeled
`provisional/osrs-lineage`. POH, post-cutoff, Daemonheim, and
location-specific contexts remain excluded. See
`source-audits/slayer-steel-dragon-combat-2026-07-19.md`.

Kuradal's two remaining family boundaries are now source-closed: ordinary
TzHaar means Ket, Xil, Mej, and Hur while Living rock creatures means
patriarch, protector, and striker. The 36-row hidden registry was not encoded
because the current schema cannot represent Kuradal's master-specific
Aquanite/Ice-strykewyrm gates, its non-quest Barbarian Training access, or its
Kuradal-Dungeon Dark-beast context without contradicting the settled
contracts. This is an implementation-schema stop, not a design question. See
`source-audits/slayer-kuradal-family-boundaries-2026-07-19.md` and
`audits/slayer-kuradal-registry-schema-conflict-2026-07-19.md`.

Duradel/Lapalok is now the sixth completely encoded Slayer pool. Its 35
source-settled rows preserve the provisional 334-point denominator, Combat
100, Slayer 50, and Shilo Village gate, plus the While Guthix Sleeps
Duradel-to-Lapalok presentation swap. The role remains runtime-hidden. The
live readiness audit derives 14/35 families ready and names all 21 missing
Combat or distinctive-mechanic blockers; no incomplete row is exposed or
renormalized. Governing evidence:
`source-audits/slayer-duradel-lapalok-pool-2026-07-17.md`.

Sumona is now the fifth completely encoded Slayer pool. Its 37 source-settled
rows preserve the provisional 407-point denominator, Combat 85, Slayer 35, and
Smoking Kills gates while remaining runtime-hidden. The live readiness audit
derives 20/37 families ready. It explicitly keeps the existing Abyssal demon
blocked on missing teleport-interruption behavior and Nechryael blocked on its
missing Death-spawn hook; 15 other families still lack a certifying Combat
target. No row is renormalized away and no owner design was reopened.

Aquanite and Desert strykewyrm are harvested but remain source-blocked. Both
Darkan definitions are explicitly `nostats` and generate non-real combat
levels. Aquanite lacks its prayer-negating handler/trigger and conflicts on
Water-rune, Seaweed, and Soul-rune quantities. Desert strykewyrm conflicts on
burrow frequency, conditional double-hit semantics, Potato-cactus/herb/Coin
quantities, while its Focus-sight rate remains source-unclosed. Neither target
or asset was added; Sumona remains 20/37. See
`source-audits/slayer-aquanite-desert-strykewyrm-combat-2026-07-19.md`.

Terror dogs are harvested but remain source-blocked. The cutoff record and
Darkan close the level-110 representative, real combat levels, route,

drop set. Darkan's executable selector nevertheless assigns five of 128 slots
to Coins ×11, while all 329 pre-cutoff article revisions permit only ×44–200
and later ×440. The talk history does not repair the conflict. No target,
Prayer helper, item, or asset was added; Sumona remains 20/37. See
`source-audits/slayer-terror-dog-combat-2026-07-19.md`.

The Spiritual-mage and Spiritual-warrior queue is stopped on a second direct

registers only one Saradomin target for each family. Rev-727 independently
contains four attackable identities per generic family. No representative rule
was inferred and no registry or Combat row was changed. See
`audits/slayer-spiritual-families-registry-conflict-2026-07-19.md`.

Red dragons are Sumona's twentieth certifying family. The ordinary level-152

conflicting 144-LP lead under the source hierarchy. See
`source-audits/slayer-red-dragon-family-combat-2026-07-19.md`.

Scabaras lancer is harvested but remains source-blocked. The cutoff page and

The Sumona queue is stopped at Mutated jadinkos on a direct settled-record/code

Earth warrior now carries the owner-approved Wilderness Combat risk instead of
being a risk-free Vannaka representative. Every successful review has the
labeled 1%/250-LP bounded ambush chance; a trigger resets the monster
encounter. Fatal ambushes keep the three highest-value equipped items, add one
with active Protect Item, retain ordinary inventory, permanently lose the rest
of the equipped risk set, and create no new grave. A process-local fingerprint
requires explicit current-risk confirmation after Anki restart or a material

Warriors' Guild is now live as the honest executable boundary for

(<https://vsx8a7pxsimj.postplan.dev>).

Mazchna/Achtryn is now the second fully represented Slayer-master pool, but it

Shilo Village is now a complete live Slayer dependency. Eleven review-native

Turael/Spria is now the first complete live Slayer-master role. The full

Rockslug and Gargoyle are now executable Slayer Combat targets rather than

The personal-fork-versus-public-release question is settled as **prepare a
separately named public derivative release**, not upload immediately. Wilson Yeh
must be credited prominently as the original creator; Arman is the independent
maintainer of the radically expanded derivative. The release uses a new
AnkiWeb listing, an explicit AGPL-3.0-or-later compatible license, durable
credits, synchronized public source, a corrected review link, and closed asset
provenance and release-engineering gates. The governing decision, measurement,
copy, outreach note, blockers, and ordered go-live script are in
`public-release-lineage-and-license-2026-07-17.md`.

The shared catalog search seam is implemented across Shop, Quests, and Combat.
`CatalogFilterBar` standardizes dark-mode-safe search, clear/Escape behavior,
page-owned filters, and count summaries without owning result semantics.
Combat search preserves the saved target, expands matching groups, and cannot
activate an absent or empty row; Quest text search composes with every existing
catalog filter. The `Current Slayer task only` checkbox remains correctly
deferred until authentic assignment state exists instead of shipping a dead
control. The complete pure suite passes all 1,478 methods; real offscreen Qt
discovery also passes all 1,478 methods with no failures or skips. The closure
report is `status-updates/slayer-search-seam-results-2026-07-17.html`, with a
mobile-safe copy at <https://s1kaasu9wfmq.postplan.dev>.

Live cadence activation and the Design 2 reconciliation are verified and ready
for their atomic commit. The post-review complete core suite passes all 1,476
methods (1,275 ordinary passes and 201 environment skips); the matching real
offscreen Qt suite passes all 1,476 methods with no skips. The
focused cadence/kernel/parity/combat/runtime/Summoning/Thieving set passes 363
methods, and no cadence test is skipped or marked as an expected failure. The
final independent review caught and closed a successful-answer disengagement
inversion that could have allowed Dream healing between every live Combat
slice; Again now remains the only answer path that marks disengagement, and its
next successful slice resumes identical combat/RNG phase. Documentation,
compilation, reachability, asset, worktree-safety, and test-quality gates are
clean. The current Anki process predates this candidate, so the next ordinary
Anki restart is the manual live-load smoke rather than a reason to disrupt an
active review session during closure. The closure report is
`status-updates/cadence-kernel-live-results-2026-07-17.html`, with a mobile
copy at <https://nl2rgoy9pvqk.postplan.dev>.

Summoning is now the eighteenth complete skill surface. The 2026-07-17 closure
ships the full ordinary pouch/scroll catalog, training, follower lifecycle,
points, BoB cargo, Familiarisation, the freeze-date pet family, restoration
potions, all 78 Bogrog exchanges, Pack yak Winter Storage, and the source-ready
boost/heal/escape effects. Daemonheim remains outside the overworld. Remaining
combat-scroll, forager, and teleport rows are explicit source/dependency blocks,
not exposed approximations, and no owner-level Summoning design gap remains.
Closure verification passes 1,446 core methods (1,242 ordinary passes, 201
environment skips, three expected failures) and 1,446 real offscreen Qt methods
(1,443 ordinary passes, three expected failures), plus focused Bogrog/follower
UI, docs, test-quality, compilation, JSON, reachability, generator-determinism,
diff, and worktree-safety checks. The reachability report records 1,220 actions
and 290 known acquisition/dependency orphans (112 root, 178 cascade); it remains
an explicit debt ledger rather than a green gate.

The active non-Summoning grill-harvest follow-up has closed its final two owner
questions. Mage Arena now has final-form alignment, matching first cape/staff,
review-native per-spell attunement, one-cape statue replacement/reclaim, and
Charge's matching-loadout +100 LP god-spell maximum-hit effect. Farming
auto-cure now saves a per-patch `Plant cure first` / `Cure Plant first`
preference, snapshots it into presets and queued steps, and falls back
atomically through the canonical item or Magic transaction. Config v40
preserves legacy Mage Arena completions and normalizes both new schemas. The
final candidate passes 1,388 core methods and 1,388 real offscreen Qt methods;
test quality, documentation, compilation, JSON, reachability, asset provenance,
diff, and worktree-safety gates pass.

The earlier loop also closed Magic Charge review duration (`6639a48`), Bank
quantity controls (`8426fa2`), the corrected Charge source boundary
(`8b6a561`), Agility carried risk (`69855d9`), and Farming replacement/preset
planning (`9b65f45`). The former open design ledger is now archived at
`archive/2026-07/resolved-queues/design-gaps-grill-harvest-2026-07-17.md`.
The owner released Summoning implementation after the final Winter Storage fork
closed at `d7539c9`.

Slayer's source-first owner grill is design-closed and integrated into `main`.
The governing record is `slayer-skill-contract-2026-07-15.md`, supported by
focused audits for master succession/challenges, finishing blows, locations,
points/rewards, Social Slayer, and the post-cutoff VIP-ticket exclusion. Live
assignment gameplay remains closed, but source-ready Combat dependencies are
now landing atomically: Cockatrice protection is complete, the finisher
supplies are obtainable, and Jackal now supplies an executable Dogs-family
variant. The ordinary level-13 Icefiend now supplies a second missing family
without folding in its God Wars, Ghorrock, quest, Revenant, or Daemonheim
forms. The Jackal slice passes the complete 1,481-method core suite, 337
focused Combat/runtime methods, and a real offscreen Qt picker check. The
Icefiend checkpoint passes all 1,482 core methods, 339 focused methods, and the
same real offscreen Qt picker path. Banshee protection is now executable too:
an unprotected scream applies the period damage shape and full eight-skill set
through a deliberately labeled flat-five abstraction; equipped Earmuffs or
Slayer-helmet-family headgear suppress it. Desert lizard is the first live
finisher family: Slayer 22, its Ice-cooler 1–30 LP gate, the 400-point
automatic-finisher flag, one exact ordinary drop selector, and a sourced
sprite are executable. Desert heat is a labeled review-native supply abstraction: each
damaging Combat review consumes one Waterskin dose, preserves the lower-dose
skin, and uses the ordinary two-review bank refill. Exact cutoff heat
timing/damage remains `source_blocked`; no wall-clock mechanic was introduced.

The first master-pool harvest is now recorded in
`source-audits/slayer-turael-spria-pool-2026-07-17.md`. Reconciliation recovered
23 ordinary Turael/Spria families, including the master-table omissions
Gelatinous abominations and Grotworms, and rejected the anonymous Troll row.
Hidden weights remain explicitly provisional; the 355-point denominator
converges across Darkan's raw values, game-main's percentages, and the recovered
30-weight Gelatinous family. Jackal closes the Dogs-family target, and the
cutoff level-13 Icefiend closes Icefiends with 150 LP, 15 Slayer XP, a 20 LP
maximum hit, and its complete 128-slot ordinary drop table. Runtime
certification still fails: Desert lizards are now executable, three families
still have no Combat target, and Gelatinous
abominations lack the worn-gauntlet finisher. None of these findings creates an
owner design question.

The second master-pool harvest is recorded in
`source-audits/slayer-mazchna-achtryn-pool-2026-07-17.md`. It reconciles a
27-family functional pool and a provisional 325-point hidden-weight
denominator. The earlier Knowledge Base correction, Achtryn's cutoff table, and
Darkan close the malformed Mazchna `40–85` range at `40–70`; Cyclopes remain
`30–60`, the repaired cutoff row span closes Grotworms at `40–70`, and Wall
beasts remain `10–20`. Mogre and Wall-beast weights are explicit 10-point
common-baseline abstractions because both emulator task-family entries are
stubs. Runtime certification still fails: ten families have no Combat target,
three existing
families lack required Slayer/protection behavior, and no assignment-family
registry exists. This pass repaired Cave crawler's omitted source
`poisonDamage = 8`. No owner design question emerged.

The third master-pool harvest is recorded in
`source-audits/slayer-vannaka-pool-2026-07-17.md`. It reconciles the cutoff
35-family pool and a provisional 460-point denominator. Darkan's unsupported
Troll row is replaced by the cutoff Mogre and Sea-snake families; their
10-point values are labeled common-baseline abstractions. Grotworms use the
ordinary `60–120` Darkan range rather than treating the cutoff page's single
observed assignment of 69 as a complete range. game-main's explicit 2.17%
ordinary weights independently round from `10 / 460`, although its live input
table remains intentionally partial. Runtime certification still fails: 23
families have no Combat target, present targets lack several required
protective/quest mechanics, and no stable assignment-family registry exists.
The same pass corrected the Stronghold Shade to 3.5 Slayer XP and one-quarter
ordinary combat/Constitution damage XP. No owner design question emerged.

The fourth master-pool harvest is recorded in

The fifth master-pool harvest is recorded in
`source-audits/slayer-sumona-pool-2026-07-17.md`. It reconciles Sumona's 37
ordinary families and a provisional 407-point denominator. Darkan supplies 35
executable rows totaling 387; cutoff evidence restores its commented

The settled design keeps assignments and exact qualifying Combat variants
source-shaped, places `Assignment`, `Masters`, and `Rewards` in one Slayer page,
deep-links into Combat as the sole fight surface, and requires a reusable list
search/filter seam. It preserves quest-reactive masters, authentic task sizes,
mandatory finishers and protective consequences, points/streaks/challenges,
target-local Slayer contexts, ring-of-slaying trip value, and runtime-ready
family activation without dead rows or renormalized incomplete master pools.
Smoking Kills, Shilo Village, and the full black-mask/Slayer-helmet dependency
chains belong to the Slayer implementation program; Social Slayer remains
parked until genuine multiplayer state exists.

Summoning's owner grill is closed and its implementation is live in canonical
`main`. The governing ledger is `summoning-skill-contract-2026-07-16.md`,
backed by the catalog, lifecycle, training, Familiarisation, pet, potion,
Bogrog, familiar-effect, Winter Storage, simulation-time, reviewer-alert, and
Help audits/contracts. Foragers preserve the period distinction between
drop-to-player output and separate familiar storage: a full carried inventory suppresses
a drop roll with a visible log entry and no queue or Bank detour, while storage-type
familiars can continue to their sourced capacity of 30.
Familiar teleports are now resolved as target-local alternate route profiles with
route-assumption metadata. Spirit graahk's sourced 60-second Nature circuit replaces the
slower sourced route rather than stacking another discount; ordinary teleports do not
consume fresh pouches, and Void shifter reuses lethal-versus-sub-10%-LP retreat semantics
without an invented cooldown. Magic teleports and charged jewellery now share those
target-local route profiles while pure Magic teleport training remains available.

Pre-EOC Summoning pets are now restored as an atomic family. **Let out** and **Pick up**
operate through Bank/the canonical follower panel; Drop is not overloaded, while permanent
release is a separately confirmed destructive action. Pets share the global follower slot,
grow and become hungry only while active, and connect directly to Construction's parked
Menagerie function. Casual pet care automatically packs a source-derived compatible-food
reserve into real carried slots and feeds on answered-card ticks, preserving inventory cost
without Tamagotchi-style manual management. Cats retain staged quest and exceptional-
function identity. Stored hunger, growth, and stage persist but freeze; letting the pet out
reuses the ordinary Casual packing boundary and feeds from real carried food before the
first hunger advance. An unfed pet automatically returns home with `Out of pet food`;
starvation never silently destroys it.

Pet acquisition is distributed to the real source systems: shops/services, quests, Hunter,
egg sources and per-pet hatch flows, Construction, and activity reward shops. A searchable
Pets log explains and links those routes but never grants pets itself. Exact costs/rates/
timers remain source-harvest work. Discontinued event pets remain compatibility-only
outside the obtainable log and completion counts. Individual page review found no
supported unreclaimable-one-time-pet category; known pets have replacement routes, while
completed Ex-ex-parrot and Broav reacquisition remain `source_blocked`. Release never
claims `Cannot be reobtained` from source silence and cannot ship for a pet whose
reacquisition row is unresolved. Platypus release-for-reward is a distinct contextual
Hunter/quest action, while generic Release never grants a reward. Cat ratting is an
explicit source-shaped target rather than ambient output; Casual wily-cat retraining
automates but preserves its interaction-review cost, and Tooth creature auto-care never
silently selects the decayed adult. Source-timed pet growth that removes a capability now
uses the already-settled reviewer expiration-alert strip without pausing the transition.
Mature god-bird protection is recorded as a source-shaped, stage-gated faction flag but
remains numerically inert until a later dedicated multi-enemy/God Wars grill; no generic
defensive buff or ambient-damage abstraction is invented now. Unsafe player death follows
the period pet-loss rule: an active pet is destroyed, and an unprotected carried pet item
is not recoverable from a grave. Banked/Menagerie pets and safe-death contexts remain
safe. The anti-Tamagotchi abstraction is limited to passive hunger/attention upkeep.
Rat Catchers' King Rat stage follows the existing quest abstraction: one status line says
the cat fights through the wall, and the stage keeps the source-required cat item gate.
Optional food, cautious retreat, LP exchange, and cat death remain represented scene detail,
not a pet-combat actor, stance selector, hidden roll, or false mandatory food gate.

The 35 settled decisions now govern answered-card lifetime and passive world ticks,
separate familiar/point/special resources, target-policy automation, carried
pouch/scroll budgets, coalesced restore and Bank detours, safe replacement and
dismissal, source-gated familiar combat, BoB inventory/reclaim rules, forbidden
area consequences, Familiarisation's four-answer session, ordinary pouch/scroll
training, and scroll-use XP. Summoning also establishes one offline Help corpus
for contextual and full-reference surfaces. The ordinary skill surface ships;
explicit source/dependency blocks remain parked as recorded in its design-gap
and source-audit ledgers.

Pack Yak's Winter Storage trigger is settled at the full-pack boundary. Casual spends one
carried scroll and 12 special points to bank one configured eligible item only when doing
so prevents the next Bank trip, then continues the target action atomically. Advanced may
choose item priority and an earlier threshold. Any target rate must declare whether Winter
Storage is already assumed.

The implementation-gap grill settled Bunyip at one sourced 20-LP heal tick per
answered-card world tick, including Again. Familiar lifetime keeps its separate
ratio-preserving calibration; effect density follows Combat's per-exchange action loop.

## Crucible Supreme Champion world-clock correction — 2026-08-08

Cutoff Crucible oldid `6538845` states the half-hour event interval, while
launch-update oldid `5778674` separately fixes its two-and-a-half-minute shape.
The settled 240-card world/economy day maps half an hour to exactly five
answered cards. Rev-727 exposes no server event clock and Darkan implements no
Supreme Champion event, so neither overrides that translation.

The shipped constant was already five, but the runtime compared it with a
visit-local productive-review counter. Events therefore paused outside the
arena and restarted on re-entry. The live event window now reads the shared
answered-card clock that advances before foreground dispatch. Persisted sessions
keep the event card and champion chosen at that boundary; old private countdown
fields sanitize away. A regression begins a visit after three world cards,
proves the event arrives on the visit's second review, drives the real runtime,
and proves a promotion during the event cannot retroactively select the player
as champion. Verification passes 41 focused Crucible/register tests, all 3,860
plain-discovery tests with 479 intentional skips, and all 3,860 real
offscreen-Qt tests with 3 intentional skips. Documentation audits 520 Markdown
and 431 HTML files; test-quality audits 3,826 tests across 176 files with zero
hard issues; diff-whitespace and worktree-layout audits pass. No owner design
gap surfaced.

## Crucible target-level matching correction — 2026-08-08

Cutoff Crucible oldid `6538845` preserves both the intended similar-combat
match and the observed failure that a relatively low-combat player could face
a maxed opponent. Its complete pre-cutoff article and talk histories contain no
numeric pair or range. Rev-727 has no matching table or target selector, and
Darkan's complete Crucible history never assigns a target or computes a combat
band. The exact spread and frequency therefore remain source-blocked.

The old registered `-8/0/+8/+20` cycle could not reproduce that attested
failure: the combat-60 entry gate topped out at level 80. The corrected cycle
keeps three ordinary near-level opponents and uses `+78`, clamped to 3–138, so
the same combat-60 player can reach level 138. The fourth-slot frequency is a
disclosed AnkiScape reachability choice, not a period claim. A regression drives
the real target constructor and proves the ordinary and severe boundaries.

## Crucible bounty-fee scale correction — 2026-08-03

The surviving period evidence proves the prior live curve was too small by
roughly one order of magnitude. Launch-day talk oldid `5778707` reports a
combat-60 range of 50,000 coins with rune-or-better gear to 500,000 with
nothing risked. The immutable 24 June fee-interface image records total
535,362, split into 486,693 risk expectation and 48,669 abandonment, while
cutoff Crucible oldid `6538845` reports roughly 518,000 for a maxed unarmed
player after the launch hotfix. Rev-727 interface 1298 confirms the three-part
shape but exposes no calculation, and Darkan has no fee implementation.

The disclosed reachability curve now uses a 450,000 risk baseline at combat 60,
adds 400 per level above 60, and uses a 50,000 abandonment component. It yields
the sourced 500,000 launch-day boundary and 531,200 at max combat, inside the
later 518,000–535,362 observations. Both values remain registered placeholders
because this is not the recovered server formula. A regression fixes the
combat-60 boundary. The focused Crucible and placeholder-register lane passes
38 tests. Full discovery passes all 3,804 tests in both lanes, with 460 expected
plain skips and 3 Qt skips. Documentation audits 515 Markdown and 428 HTML
files; test quality audits 3,770 tests with 0 hard issues. Diff-hygiene and
worktree audits pass. No owner design gap surfaced.

Conquest is a safe persistent Activities match against a labelled bounded
simulated opponent. Combat level 40 gates entry. Casual uses the reconstructed
default ten-troop formation and four commands; Advanced exposes the complete
seven-unit source roster, 1,000-point budget, ten-unit placeholder cap, up to four
of all nine exact commands, and balanced/aggressive/defensive doctrines. The
persisted board uses the settled 20×20 placeholder geometry and seeded obstacle
layouts. One productive review runs one troop action for each side through the
same bounded heuristic, with exact movement, range, damage, LP, command effects,
cooldowns, normal-kill command-point income, Bombard's zero income, and Chastise's
last-troop restriction. Twenty seeded reducer matches all terminate within 60
exchanges.

Elimination and direct resignation settle into Pest Control's shared commendation
wallet. The monotone 1–10 placeholder curve uses the exact ×2/×3/×4 combat bands
and 20/30/40 caps, pays both outcomes without a win bonus, and respects the shared
500-point cap. Rating starts at 1,000, floors at zero, uses a registered
opponent-sensitive placeholder formula, and unlocks the 150-commendation Void
knight deflector purchase at 1,250. The deflector's sourced equipment row and both
period Music Log tracks are live.

Migration and repair, activity arbitration, cadence, telemetry, review/direct
undo, activity/equipment/item registration, normalized art and provenance, and
the compact three-page Activities card are integrated. The correction guard tests
pin Knight range 1, Mage range 4, Champion 500 LP, Stoicism 75 CP, and the absence
of a combat triangle or hybrid roster. Eighteen in-scope placeholders are
registered. The quest-only pest battle and Torcher candidate, staked mode,
human-only truce/disconnect flows, and Void-set substitution semantics remain
outside this slice.

Focused verification passes 137 backend tests, 3 direct/review undo integration
tests, 2 real offscreen-Qt card tests, and compilation of the affected package
surfaces. Source authority:
`source-audits/conquest-harvest-2026-07-28.md`. Results:
`status-updates/conquest-breadth-one-results-2026-07-30.html` and
<https://859ao4lbxil1.postplan.dev>.


## Gnome Restaurant and gnome cooking — 2026-07-28

Reached breadth level one in the `families/gnome-restaurant` worktree, schema 97.

**Gnome cooking is real Cooking.** Twenty period dishes ship as Cooking targets
with exact levels (6 to 42), exact XP (50 to 190) and the exact ingredient list of
both period assembly stages, consumed in one action the way the pie and pizza prep
bridges already work. Seven knife bridges turn fruit into the slices and chunks the
recipes call for. Every ingredient was already reachable: Grand Tree Groceries and
Funch's stock the dough, spice, leaves, tins, trays, moulds, shaker, glass and
spirits, King worm and Swamp toad come from pickpocketing gnomes, and Herblore
already removes toad's legs. The thirteen dishes are edible with the healing values
2011Scape's own food table gives them, and the seven cocktails are drinkable with
their sourced Strength boost and Attack drain through a new flat-level drink
consumable.

**The delivery loop.** One productive review delivers the outstanding order and
takes the next job, so the loop needs no direct action between deliveries. `Again`
never destroys an order: it spends six world minutes of the order's own source
window, and what survives becomes the chance of the good tip. Nineteen easy and
twenty hard customers ship complete with their exact quest and Magic gates, easy
jobs pay one point and hard jobs three, twelve points pay the Reward token and each
further twelve banks another free meal up to ten, and all thirty-seven sourced tip
rows are reachable — including the Gnome scarf and goggles, which only Captain
Ninto and Captain Daerkin ever hand over. The entry gate reads durable Cooking
production, so four dishes and four cocktails cooked at any time in the past open
the service without a single tutorial-only review.

Five registered placeholders cover the burn endpoints, the order-request weights,
the tip weights, the speed-to-tip curve, and the token's food draw. The presentation
fork deferred since 2026-07-17 is queued as design decision 3.19 with both layouts
drawn; the recommended one is implemented. Durable record:
`source-audits/gnome-restaurant-harvest-2026-07-28.md`.


## Dominion Tower landed — 2026-07-28

Branch `families/dominion-tower`, schema 98 (the HUD strip merged as 95 and two
sibling worktrees hold 96 and 97). 3,368 tests pass in both lanes, up from
3,301. One finding worth carrying: copying an NPC's combat level into all four
combat stats is wrong for high-level bosses, because a RuneScape combat level is
a composite — it gave Nomad a defence of 699. Temple Trekking's 72% core-stat
ratio is the house answer and the tower now uses it too.

Adjacent bug found and not fixed (it belongs to Fight Caves): `fight_caves_logic`
zeroes `combat_auto_flee_percent` around its burst to stop the auto-flee, but the
engine reads the boolean `combat_auto_flee`. The Fight Cave therefore still
flees silently when the player runs out of food.
**Correction 2026-08-22:** this bug is fixed — `logic/fight_caves_logic.py:514-531`
now saves, suspends, and restores the boolean `combat_auto_flee` around its burst;
the obsolete percent key is no longer touched.

## Clan Wars horizontal closure — 2026-07-26

- Finished the interrupted `711a87c8` build. Both free-for-all portals and the
  purple team race ship as explicit modes with a single bounded eight-review
  match shape.
- The red portal is genuinely dangerous. `_combat_apply_pvm_death` gained a
  `no_grave` mode, so a red death destroys carried and worn items, writes no
  gravestone and no Death's Office entry, and lets Protect Item save exactly
  one item. Three tests fail if that safety net is ever restored, including one
  covering the grave-stacking branch where it would most plausibly return.
- The interrupted build could not actually be played. Measured across combat
  levels 10 to 99, a match could only ever end `0-0`: the player could not kill
  a rival inside the review bound and could not be killed by one, so `win`,
  `loss`, `kill`, `death` and the whole red-portal consequence were unreachable.
  A disclosed crossfire model (the rest of the arena wearing down both the rival
  and the player) makes every outcome reachable through real play; the tests pin
  each one to a recorded seed.
- Two inventions from the interrupted build were removed rather than documented:
  a `balanced`/`pressure`/`survival` damage dial that no source supports and
  whose descriptions promised an unimplemented defence trade, and three OpenRS2
  object IDs in `CLAN_WARS_SOURCE` that appear nowhere in the Darkan tree.
- The red portal's boost caps were nearly registered as a placeholder, then
  found exactly in Darkan `Potions.kt` `checkPVPPotionBoosts`. They ship as an
  exact sourced value with a literal-pinning test. No placeholder was added.
- Adjacent defects fixed: the enemy registry row had a hallucinated schema with
  no `stats`, and Clan Wars was missing from the once-per-review multiplier
  exclusion list, so one answer at multiplier 10 consumed a whole match.
- The panel now renders flags only; `clan_wars_view_pure` owns every enable,
  visibility and warning decision including portal selectability and the
  red-portal danger warning.
- Verification passes both full lanes at 2,780 tests: non-Qt with 334 expected
  skips and real offscreen Qt with one intentional skip.
- Durable evidence:
  `source-audits/clan-wars-horizontal-implementation-2026-07-26.md`. One design
  fork is open in `design-thread-queue.md` section 3.1.

## Slayer Wall beast Combat slice (2026-07-18)

The ordinary level-49 Wall beast is now an executable Mazchna-family Combat
target. A real Spiny helmet is required only to start the fight: a player may
swap headgear after discovery and continue the active encounter, but killing
that beast while unhelmeted cannot chain into another spawn during the same
review. This is a distinct `start_equipment` contract, not the per-enemy-attack
damage/drain behavior used by Banshee and Cockatrice.

The target carries the cutoff level, 1,050 LP, Slayer 35, 105 Slayer XP,
44-LP maximum hit, poison immunity, and assignment identity; Darkan contributes
exact combat stats, 39-tick respawn, and the thirty-row drop distribution.
Ancient Smoke spells therefore deal their ordinary hit without their extra
poison damage. The drop distribution is normalized losslessly to 100,000 slots
with ten empty slots, and every item maps to an existing canonical registry
identity. The pre-cutoff Wall beast icon is installed with provenance.

Mazchna/Achtryn readiness is now 24/27. Only Cyclopes, Mogres, and Vampyres
remain. No owner design gap emerged. Durable evidence is
`source-audits/slayer-wall-beast-combat-2026-07-18.md`; the visual closure
report is `status-updates/slayer-wall-beast-combat-results-2026-07-18.html`
(<https://0aaltfsrmyrn.postplan.dev>).

The next Mazchna blocker, Mogres, is now source-closed but correctly remains
dependency-blocked. Cutoff evidence pins the level-60 target, atomic
Fishing-explosive lure, Slayer 32/48 XP, poison immunity, six-tick melee,
89-LP maximum hit, the exact three-stage Skippy supply chain, and the unlock.
Darkan supplies matching combat stats, a 59-tick respawn, an exact 128-slot
main drop table, and the shared long/curved-bone tertiary table. The existing
Slayer family already requires completion ID `skippy_and_the_mogres`, but that
miniquest is absent from the quest catalog. Adding a permanently locked target
would falsely advance readiness, so the quest-owned chain is handed to the
Darkan quest thread and no runtime row ships yet. No owner design gap emerged.
Durable evidence is
`source-audits/slayer-mogre-combat-2026-07-18.md`.

implemented skill by one current level. Runtime messaging distinguishes bite
healing from passive regeneration.

Mazchna/Achtryn readiness is now 25/27. Mogres remain dependency-blocked by the
quest-owned Skippy miniquest; Cyclopes remain dependency-blocked by the complete
Warriors' Guild token/defender activity. No owner design gap emerged. Durable
evidence is
`source-audits/slayer-feral-vampyre-combat-2026-07-18.md`.

## Slayer Cockatrice and Banshee protection slices (2026-07-17)

Cockatrice now preserves the cutoff Mirror-shield consequence instead of
behaving like an ordinary harmless melee target. Missing the equipped shield
causes an automatically landed fixed hit using the labeled
maximum-LP/6-with-110-cap reconciliation and drains Attack, Strength, Defence,
Ranged, and Agility by one quarter of base per attack down to the period-proven
one-quarter floor. The ordinary Combat food, retreat, death, grave, persistence,
and review-undo paths remain authoritative.

The existing Combat picker warns without hard-blocking either legal dangerous
encounter. Cockatrice clears only for a Mirror shield in the shield slot.
Banshee clears only for Earmuffs, masked earmuffs, or a Slayer-helmet-family
item in the head slot. Its fixed maximum-LP/10 scream caps at 80 LP and drains
the period-proven eight skills by five levels per attack to level 1. The
flat-five magnitude is explicitly an owner-authorized bounded abstraction from
Darkan; the source conflict remains visible in
`source-audits/slayer-protective-equipment-cockatrice-banshee-2026-07-17.md`.

The Banshee extension passes 236 focused Combat/roster/metadata tests, one real
offscreen Qt Combat-picker check, and all 1,485 current core methods with 203
environment skips. Test quality remains at zero hard issues and the pre-existing
four structural-clone candidates; documentation, compilation, diff and
worktree-safety checks pass.

## Current Baseline — 2026-07-16

AnkiScape now has 18 complete skill surfaces, five partial Combat-trained skills, and two planned skills. Canonical live status is in `future-work-kanban.md`; the dated 2026-07-10 completion audit is archived baseline evidence.

## Always-visible reviewer strip — 2026-07-28

Built in the `hud-strip` worktree; the last unbuilt decision from pass 3 of the
playtest notes (design-thread section 4.8).

**What the strip contains, and when.** The running activity's real skill bars
appear whenever that activity can award experience on the next review, so a live
shooting star shows Mining and the bar disappears when the star resolves; the
bars come from `ACTIVITY_TRAINED_SKILLS`, which mirrors the runtime award sites,
with the Evil Tree resolving by phase and Tears of Guthix by its least-experience
reward skill. Two bars is the cap and the rest are named in the caption. The
Grand Exchange fills drawer moved out of its tab to sit under the experience
bars, appears only while a slot is occupied, opens collapsed, and keeps pass 2's
green-for-complete and yellow-for-partial rows newest first. The Distraction and
Farming badge is the one permanent seat.

**Watching is settable.** A `★`/`☆` control on the Activities detail card's
shared control row pins one Distraction. It sits there rather than in forty-odd
bespoke card headers, which is also how the five panel-class families get it for
free. The pin commits through `commit_direct_action`, so it undoes. Schema 95
adds `dnd_favourites`, sanitized against the Distraction group plus the Evil
Tree, which has no activity spec of its own.

**Boundaries kept honest.** Combat activities and the five reward-table families
show no bar, because their skill comes from the attack style or from a payout
choice. Section 3.15 records the one fork this work found — a pin adds a badge
line and never removes one — with its recommendation implemented.

**Verification.** Both lanes green from the 3251/377 baseline, up 26 tests:
`tests/test_review_strip_logic.py` (23 pure cases), `tests/test_review_strip.py`
(15 offscreen Qt cases, replacing `test_farming_hud.py` and
`test_distraction_hud.py` with every case they pinned carried over), two pin
cases in `tests/test_main_menu_widget.py`, one relocation case for the Grand
Exchange tab, and two undo-seam cases in
`tests/test_direct_action_undo_seam.py`. A light and dark offscreen pass probes
geometry rather than pixels, per the offscreen rule.

## Tears of Guthix and Enchanted Key — 2026-07-26

Both reached breadth level one in the `families/batch-c` worktree.

**Tears of Guthix.** The July audit called the exact values source-blocked, but
almost all of them were in the revision it had already pinned. Shipped exactly as
stated: the "100,000 experience or one quest point since you last participated"
gate, the tick-per-Quest-Point cavern timer capped at three minutes, `level * 6`
capped at 180 with the stone bowl and 300 with the ornate one, the least-
*experience* reward rule, and the ornate bowl's two level-80 requirements. Where
the ornate bowl article contradicts the D&D article about "five times", the audit
records the conflict and the code ships the explicit caps. The per-tick
collection rate and the green-tear chance stayed unrecoverable and are registered
placeholders. Two things are AnkiScape abstractions and are labelled as such
everywhere: one review is six game ticks (design-thread queue 3.2), and up to two
weekly visits bank under Decision 2.

**Enchanted Key.** Darkan already implements the hunt, so the pass was
corroboration: all eleven loot rows, the Chebyshev metric, the eight temperature
bands, the melt and Erin's 500-coin replacement all agree with the pinned
Enchanted key revision. Nothing needed a placeholder. The one abstraction is the
walking, and it has a consequence the audit names rather than hides — three of
the eight temperature bands cannot fire once the search is flattened to
site-to-site, and a test pins which bands are live. The three god mjolnirs join
through a family-owned item seam.

Both families follow the Skeletal Horror shape: a pure data module, a pure logic
module owning every enable and visibility flag, a panel that only renders those
flags, runtime and storage wiring, undo through `commit_direct_action`, period
art with pinned provenance, and four layers of tests that reach every reward by
constructing the state a player would actually be in.

### Adversarial verification — 2026-07-26

The entry above was written before the family was verified, and three of its
claims were wrong. Corrected here rather than in place, so the two can be
compared:

- **"weekly recurrence with two-bank carryover" was an invention.** Both source
  audits say repeat availability *resets* each simulated week. Banking two
  entries is a mechanic the period did not have. `SKELETAL_HORROR_ENTRY_CAP` is
  now `1` and the test that pinned the banking was corrected toward the source.
- **"tests that reach every reward through the real review path" was not true.**
  Every mechanics test mocked `apply_combat_burst_pure`, so nothing proved the
  fight worked at all. `SkeletalHorrorRealCombatPathTests` now drives the real
  kernel from a fresh save to victory, to a repeat victory with the elite clue,
  and to a real death.
- **"No owner-only design fork emerged" was wrong.** The post-quest wish lists
  are satisfied by a free button press; that is now design-thread queue 3.1.

Also fixed: the panel re-derived the Slayer-78 gate itself instead of reading
the view, and the phase thresholds were restated in the logic layer instead of
being read from the phase table.

Phase 1 of the cadence kernel is complete on `main` at `b45aff0`. It
adds a dormant pure scheduler, additive config-v34 route residuals, the strict
off-by-default `ankiscape_cadence_kernel` flag, and exact save/load/undo and
flag-off RNG/output regression coverage. No skill behavior is routed or changed.

Phase 3 shadow observation is merged into `main` at `6422610`. The
six in-scope adapters record current and proposed outcomes without mutating live
gameplay. The 175-route catalog has 24 source-ready routes and 151 explicit
`source_blocked` routes. Exact range envelopes, embedded Cooking/Firemaking
source rolls, overflow declarations, save/load sanitation, reporting, and
review undo are covered. Combat and the now-separately-shipped Thieving surface
remain outside the cadence experiment.

## Complete Skill Surfaces

- Gathering: Mining, Woodcutting, Farming, Fishing, Hunter.
- Artisan: Smithing, Crafting, Fletching, Firemaking, Cooking, Runecrafting, Herblore, Construction.
- Support/combat-adjacent: Prayer, Magic, Thieving, Agility, and Summoning.

These surfaces use stable IDs, flat save state, review or background dispatch as appropriate, inventory mutation, telemetry, undo, assets/provenance, Skills-hub UI, Stats/Bank/HUD integration, and focused tests.

## Partial Combat Skills

Unified Combat trains Attack, Strength, Defence, Constitution, and Ranged. They deliberately remain `partial` because they do not own independent Skills-hub review surfaces. Magic is complete: its noncombat and combat contracts both ship.

## Implemented Partial Dungeoneering

Dungeoneering is the sixth partial trainable skill. Its Frozen floor runtime,
Ring of Kinship tiers, staged reward trader, and working Coal bag are live.
Further floor themes and depth remain program work; the registry has no planned
skill surface.

## Thieving closure

Thieving adds 17 pickpocket rows, 18 stall-family rows, one complete Menaphite
blackjack row, two visible source-blocked quest rows, exact cadence scheduling,
source-weighted loot, shared combat LP/stun/fight-back state, storage v35,
grouped Qt selection, four stable achievements, RuneScape-sourced skill and
Phoenix assets with provenance, and targeted pure/runtime/storage/Qt coverage.
Failure damage reuses Combat auto-eat/death/grave state, while encounter-only
Menaphites stay out of the ordinary Combat picker. The Feud and The Golem were
already playable in the 194-quest catalog; Thieving consumes those live

## Truth as of 2026-07-15

- `core/skill_registry.py` has 19 complete skill surfaces and six partial trainable skills; it has no planned skill surface.
- Complete: Mining, Woodcutting, Farming, Fishing, Smithing, Crafting, Fletching, Firemaking, Cooking, Runecrafting, Hunter, Herblore, Prayer, Magic, Construction, Thieving, Agility, Slayer, and Summoning.
- Partial through unified Combat: Attack, Strength, Defence, Constitution, and Ranged.
- Partial through Daemonheim floors: Dungeoneering.
- Farming is shipped (`9a88c56`), not the next build.
- The 194-row quest catalog remains coverage scaffolding rather than full quest parity.
- NPC Shops are live; the fake GE remains parked until the item/acquisition universe is representative.
- The current reachability instrument reports 1,600 actions, 2,468 reachable
  items, 36 orphan inputs (32 root and four cascade), 35 unresolved actions, and
  1,218 seed items. Queue entry 5.12 proves that inaccessible combat drops and
  shop stock can still enter this ledger, so its output is a diagnostic signal,
  not proof that a player can acquire an item.

## Bob Barter Closure — 2026-07-15

- Bob Barter (herbs) is a free, instantaneous Grand Exchange shop service. One
  click redistributes every eligible live non-Daemonheim vial-potion family to
  the selected period-supported 2-, 3-, or 4-dose size. Total doses and vial
  containers are conserved: a partial remainder is retained, splitting consumes
  owned empty vials, and consolidation returns excess vials. The action changes
  no review or clock state and records an independently undoable shop event.
- Anki was loading three linked worktrees beside the canonical checkout because
  all four lived directly under `addons21` as enabled packages. The linked
  worktrees now live under the sibling `Anki2/ankiscape_worktrees/` root, and
  `tools/worktree_guard.py` audits that runtime boundary and creates future
  worktrees only in the safe location.
- The deck-browser entry point replaces its own nested callbacks by stable hook
  key through Anki's production `_hooks` storage, so repeated registration or a
  reload leaves exactly one callback and one menu open per click.
- Ordinary Herblore now excludes the 54 Daemonheim-only clean, unfinished, and finished targets while preserving their item identities for old saves. A removed saved selection migrates to `clean_guam` without deleting inventory.
- The live Herblore catalog is 22 clean, 22 unfinished, and 69 finished targets.
  All 113 rows now use local RS3-provenanced artwork. The skill rail uses the
  compact OSRS `Herblore icon (detail).png`, matching the source/style family of
  the other skill icons instead of the modern RS3 `Herblore detail.png` art.

- Live source-ready gathering routes advance exact lower-endpoint residuals;
  Cooking and Firemaking compare one already-drawn roll to the authentic source
  probability and draw no second random value.
- Source ranges remain ranges in evidence. Live admission uses the explicitly
  labeled conservative lower endpoint rather than inventing a midpoint.
- Historical shadow residuals remain readable but never seed or advance live
  `cadence_profiles`.
- Whole-addon coverage records every source-incompatible or missing route as
  `source_blocked` while preserving its approved handler action.
- Prayer selection is atomic across material and method. Hosted/owned altar state is cleared only when that composite selection changes.
- Owned gilded altars accept zero, one, or two burners at 250%, 300%, or 350%; clean marrentill is consumed once per new stint and the multiplier remains frozen for that stint.
- Training urns use exact five-state physical identities, non-stackable semantics, target-carried activation, overflow-preserving charge, ten-nonempty caps, and direct full-urn teleport.
- Saved custom layouts follow a physical urn through `(r)` to active to full, so skilling and Combat bank runs do not strand a charging urn.
- Direct teleport awards the urn skill's XP, refreshes the visible Skills panel, persists immediately, and uses a separate LIFO custom undo rather than review undo.
- Bonecrusher and Ectoplasmator operate only from the selected target's carried pack; Bonecrusher also intercepts Hunter bone outputs. No acquisition route was invented.
- Chivalry, Rigour, Augury, and Piety enforce their exact Defence requirements; Knight Waves locks are visible in the Qt copy.
- Ordinary Bonemeal (source ID 4255) and Wyvern bonemeal (source ID 6810) remain distinct despite sharing a source display name. Super prayer consumes only the disambiguated Wyvern storage key.
- Bonecrusher and Ectoplasmator icons now use explicit RS3 wiki provenance instead of the fallback OSRS-first resolver.

## Agility closure (2026-07-15)

Agility is now a complete Support skill. It ships the full course/action-stage
engine, obstacle-specific failure/LP/setback behavior, lap bonuses, every audited
activity family, Brimhaven's review-native ticket chase, 68 ordinary/grapple
shortcuts, run-energy trip costs with actual carried weight, equipment and boost
effects, achievements, command undo, a grouped Qt surface, and period-provenanced
assets. Circus and every other cadence boundary derive from answered cards or
Agility actions; a static regression guard rejects wall-clock imports from the
gameplay modules.

The release-blocking live integrations are active: barehand Hunter butterflies,
Fishing double catches, Herblore consumable effects, Habitat traversal, quest and
lamp rewards, Farming east-patch travel, LP/death, inventory, equipment, storage,
and runtime dispatch. Future-owner hooks remain explicit but inert for Summoning,
Slayer, Dungeoneering, full Blast Furnace, Livid Farm, diary acquisition,
Barbarian Assault, and quest-owned stages. Exact Dorgesh route identities are
registered; still-hidden payout differences remain `source_blocked` rather than
invented. See
`memory-bank/source-audits/agility-implementation-verification-2026-07-15.md`.

## Cadence Phase 3 Closure — 2026-07-13

The cadence Phase 3 closure adds:

- one guarded central observation seam for Mining, Woodcutting, Fishing, Hunter, Cooking, and Firemaking;
- exact rational low/high source envelopes with no midpoint collapse;
- reuse of the already-consumed live roll for Cooking and Firemaking, with no RNG drift;
- a separate transactional `cadence_shadow` namespace that never seeds live `cadence_profiles`;
- complete 175-route catalog and observed-save reporters for current, proposed, and source XP/hour;
- fail-closed handling for 151 missing or semantically incompatible source rows;
- fixed-seed flag-on/off gameplay-byte, XP, loot, call-sequence, RNG, save/load, and undo proof.

The durable closure record is `cadence-kernel-phase3-shadow-2026-07-13.md`.

## Miniquest ledger — stage 1 complete, 2026-07-29

Miniquests are a flag on the existing quest ledger, not a second system: same
storage, same unlock engine, same stage and journal machinery, with their own
`Miniquests` grouping on the Quests tab that only appears when both groups are
on screen. Two rows ship, exactly the two the owner named — The Hunt for Surok,
which makes the parked Bork loop reachable, and The Lair of Tarn Razorlor,
which lights the Dominion Tower class-H encounter that already gated on this
id. No catalog sweep.

Both play end to end through the ordinary reducers. Surok gates on What Lies
Below plus Mining 42 and ends on Bork; Tarn gates on Haunted Mine plus Slayer
40 and ends on Tarn's two forms. Each awards the sourced 5,000 Slayer XP and no
quest points.

Two lessons worth keeping. First, the quest catalog builder reads the wiki's
quest release list, which carries no miniquests at all, so any miniquest row
has to be written by hand and appended to `QUESTS`. Second, an enemy's
RuneScape combat level must never be copied into its four core stats: Bork at
level 267 was both unhittable and unsurvivable until the row adopted the
already-registered 72%-of-level conversion the Dominion Tower work settled for
the same problem.

Schema 103. Sources and the recorded data-depth gaps — the whole salve amulet
chain, Tarn's diary as an item, and the still-parked Bork daily loop — are in
`memory-bank/source-audits/miniquest-ledger-2026-07-29.md`.

Stage 2 closed the sourcing: every requirement, stage and reward was re-read
against the pinned revisions and against Darkan, and nothing moved. Two
requirements were considered and deliberately left out — Prayer 43, which
Darkan's miniquest enum enforces but the same wiki table it came from annotates
"not needed to complete the quest", and Slayer 44, which appears on one page
only and gates nothing that must be killed. Darkan's Bork is level 107 with a
1,500 XP daily, both post-cutoff figures; the pre-cutoff record's 267 and 250
win. Design thread 3.23 is closed, and its claim that the Tarn boss belongs to
no glove set was re-checked and now has a test.

## Bork, the daily Chaos Tunnels encounter — 2026-07-30

The loop the 2026-07-18 audit parked is live. Bork is a Distraction and
Diversion: one kill per answered-card day, gated on the `hunt_for_surok`
miniquest the ledger just made completable, fought through the ordinary
six-tick live Combat slices. At 2,000 of his 3,000 life points he calls three
to nine Ork legions, which cannot be engaged and press from range every review,
and the lair's Dagon'hai Elite lands a 100-250 magic bolt every 33 ticks that
Protect from Magic answers. A victory pays 250 Slayer XP, 2,000-10,000 coins
and the full guaranteed bundle; the Varrock Elite tasks and a worn Ring of
Wealth each move the payout to its own sourced column.

Three findings worth keeping. The 2026-07-18 audit's `source_blocked` charm
bundle is closed: Darkan's `guaranteedDrops` reproduces the pinned page's drop
table in all four columns, so the table's 5 blue / 7 crimson / 2 green stands
and the same page's prose is simply wrong. Every kill in this loop is a repeat
kill, because the miniquest completion already pays the sourced 5,000 first-kill
Slayer XP. And a homogeneous pressure front needs no target picker at all — the
legions despawn with Bork, so there was never a choice to offer.

Sources, the omitted rateless drops and the rest of the judgements are in
`memory-bank/source-audits/bork-daily-loop-2026-07-30.md`.

  actions or registered unsatisfiable encounters, limits start inventory to
  startable quests, and takes prerequisite plus quest-point gates to a fixed
  point. It depends on `goal/acquire-2026-08-12` and must merge after it.

  Diplomacy is a real false positive because unreachable consumed mail is not

## MacBook-backed promotion evidence — 2026-08-27

- The pending UI branch's MacBook plain lane emitted `Ran 4507 tests in 247.142s` and `OK (skipped=513)`, then the old runner rejected that successful suite because 513 exceeded its absolute cap of 500. Verbose accounting confirmed that all 513 skips were legitimate.
- The same branch's MacBook Qt lane emitted `Ran 4507 tests in 444.064s` and `OK (skipped=3)`.
- Active coverage is the stable invariant: `tests - skipped` gives 3,994 active plain tests and 4,504 active Qt tests for those observations. Direct main runs measured 4,506/512 in 251.835s for plain and 4,506/3 in 446.244s for Qt, establishing discovery of 4,506 plus active floors of 3,994 plain and 4,503 Qt tests. A new Qt-only test can therefore increase plain skips without masking lost executable coverage.
- `python3 tools/promotion_gate.py run <promotion-worktree> --remote-lanes` sends only the serial plain and Qt lanes through the existing MacBook runner. Dispatches from this machine share a fixed absolute flock through sync and polling while the local process lives; the lock does not cover another machine or a detached lane after interruption or timeout. Run IDs prevent false evidence mixing, but an orphaned lane requires operator reconciliation before retry. Local audits, exact-commit evidence, locks, logs, and landing authority remain in the promotion worktree or canonical Git common directory; the unchanged landing command is `python3 tools/promotion_gate.py land <promotion-worktree>`.
- Preserved branches `goal/remote-promotion-gate-2026-08-27` at `d79e27473830fa9bd353e5a664bc32ea38d9f247` and `goal/remote-promotion-gate-revised-2026-08-27` at `faaed0895d98366d1d0db77ed1115ecc351902c5` are superseded by the final reviewed replacement, `goal/remote-promotion-gate-final-2026-08-27`. Both prior unmerged worktrees remain intact under the worktree safety rule.
- On the disposable MacBook mirror, the complete `tests.test_remote_test_host`, `tests.test_promotion_gate`, and global UI-asset normalization modules passed 37 tests in 18.305s.
